Let's analyze polynomial perturbation more systematically. We want p_i = U_i + q_i. Since U_i uniform. The objective coefficients:
c_k = T_k + L_k + R_k, where T_k triangular/n², L_k = (2/n) prefix sum of q for k<n (with boundary) or suffix, R_k=∑q_i q_{k-i}.
Uniform T_k has max at k=n-1 value (n-1)/n² ~1/n. We need max ≤ α/n with α≈0.751. L_k and R_k can be negative to reduce. Because q signed with zero sum. Since L is linear in q, R quadratic.

If q amplitude small, linear term L can adjust; max can be reduced to maybe? Choose q to minimize L+T (ignore R). ```
def optimize_start(p, n, time_budget, rng):
    p = np.clip(p, eps, None); p/=p.sum()
    best=p; best_score=score(p)
    beta = 20-100
    eta = 0.05
    for iter:
       c=conv(p,p); maxc=c.max(); score direct
       # weights: use top active lags. Use softmax of (c - top) * beta maybe.
       # Choose active threshold: w = (c >= maxc * (1 - 0.01)) maybe top fraction 0.05.
       # For smoothness, w = softmax(beta * (c - maxc) / maxc)
       # Since c max ~1/n, beta large.
       # Use weights for all 2n-1 lags, but normalization: w sums1.
       w = np.exp((c - maxc)*beta); w/=w.sum()  # beta * scale of c (c differences ~1/n)
       # If beta too high, underflow; scale diff/maxc.
       diff = (c - maxc)/maxc
       w=np.exp(beta*diff)
       # gradient for sum p=1 domain p variables (not simplex constraints)
       g = 2*n? derivative of c wrt p. If p normalized sum1, c depends on p. g_i = 2 cross_corr(w, p). If using F= sum w c, w depends on p but ignore (smooth).
       # multiplicative update: p *= exp(-eta*g*?); normalize; clip.
       # Since c degree2 and w sum? Maybe scale eta = step / (g.max()+1e-12)
```

```
def convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a)+len(b)-1
    N=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,N)*np.fft.rfft(b,N),N)[:n]
```
For p real length n, compute c length 2n-1. FFT per iter O(N log N), N power of two ~4096/8192. Faster than direct for moderate/large. But gradient cross-correlation w*p also can be computed with FFT.

Functions:
```
def fft_conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:m]
    # fix small imaginary negative
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    # for nonnegative inputs, small negative at ends maybe; set c=np.maximum(c,0) for score? But gradient with w may use negatives? Use c maybe.
    return c
```
Cross correlation `cross_corr(w,p)` y_i=∑ p_j w_{i+j}. L need next pow2 ≥ n + len(w) -1? For circular formula Z=-i values, w indices up to 2n-2; need L≥3n-2. Use pow2.
```
def cross_hankel(w,p):
    n=p.size; L=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    Z=np.fft.irfft(P*np.conj(W),L)
    idx = np.r_[0, L-1:L-n]  # length n? For i=0 idx0=0, i=1 L-1, ..., i=n-1 L-n+1
    return Z[idx]
```
Check length: For n=3, idx=[0,L-1,L-2]. Good.
Need avoid recomputing idx each time? okay.

Score: p should sum1. c=fft_conv(p,p); maxc = c.max(); score=2*n*maxc. Because p normalized. If p sum not exactly1, local score =2n*maxc/(sum²). Evaluator clips but we can normalize. Use sump=p.sum(); score=2*n*maxc/(sump*sump) to be robust. But keep normalized.

Multiplicative optimizer:
```
def refine(p, time_limit, rng, beta_stats=..., verbose_label=''):
   n=len(p)
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
   p[np.isnan...]=0; p=np.clip(p, 1e-12, None); p=p/p.sum()
   best=p; best_score=score(p)
   # beta: use logsumexp/active K
   # We'll maintain beta schedule from 5 to 200.
   beta=10.0
   eta_choices = np.array([2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1])
   top_frac=0.05
   while time.time()<limit:
      c=fft_conv(p,p)
      maxc=c.max(); cur_score=2*n*maxc
      if cur_score<best_score: best=p; best_score=cur_score; print...
      # active weights: choose k = int(max(4, len(c)*top_frac))
      k = max(1, int(len(c)*top_frac))
      # threshold via partition (avoid sort)
      if k < len(c):
         thresh = np.partition(c, len(c)-k)[-k]
      else: thresh = 0
      # use softmax over active with beta scaled by maxc: w = exp(beta*(c - thresh)/maxc) for c>=thresh else 0
      w = np.zeros(len(c))
      mask = c >= thresh
      if mask.sum()==0: mask[maxc index]=True
      scale = maxc if maxc>0 else 1
      w[mask] = np.exp(beta*(c[mask]-thresh)/scale)
      w /= w.sum()
      # gradient
      y=cross_hankel(w,p)
      # change dimensionless delta = eta * y / (y.max()+eps)
      y_max = y.max()
      if y_max >0:
          delta = y/y_max
      else: delta=np.zeros(n)
      # Try a few eta and random noise
      candidates = []
      for eta in eta_choices:
          q = p * np.exp(-eta*delta)
          # add small random multiplicative noise sometimes
          if rng.random()<0.3:
             q *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.02, n))
          q = np.clip(q, 1e-12, None)
          q /= q.sum()
          sc = score(q)
          if sc < best_score:
             # accept best candidate (not trial)
             best=q; best_score=sc; p=q; break
      else:
          # if no improvement, try smaller eta or random block
          # adaptive: if many no-improve, increase noise, change beta
          beta *= 1.1
          if beta>500: beta=50
          # Try a random block perturbation on best
          if rng.random()<0.2:
             q=best.copy()
             width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//100), n//5)); start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
             q[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3))
             q=np.clip(q,1e-12,None); q/=q.sum()
             sc=score(q); if sc<best_score: p=q; best=q; best_score=sc
      # avoid infinite if no progress
```

```
def optimize_packing(q, n, budget, rng):
   q=corner(n)
   best=None; best_score=2*n / q.sum()**2 (since max C ~1)
   # ensure maxC <= maybe normalized to1
   while time:
      C=conv(q,q); M=C.max()
      if M>0: q = q / sqrt(M)  # max 1
      S=q.sum(); score=2*n/(S*S)
      if score<best: update
      # active weights: use C (max1) with beta maybe large; top fraction
      w=...
      V=2*cross(w,q)  # length n
      # If V all positive. KKT target constant.
      # relative correction disp = log(mean(V)/V)
      disp = np.log(np.mean(V)/V)
      # use alpha small 0.05-0.2
      for alpha in [0.3,0.15,0.05]:
          q_new = q * np.exp(alpha*disp)  # if V high, disp negative
          # ensure positive; Cmax maybe not <=1? Need check new max C/S²? Actually constraints C≤? q_new may violate max1.
          Cnew=conv(q_new,q_new)
          if Cnew.max() <= 1*(1+1e-9) maybe accept if Snew higher; else scale down? We can choose alpha if feasible and S increases.
          # To maintain constraint, if infeasible scale q_new by 1/sqrt(maxCnew) and compare score.
```
Since we always normalize by sqrt(M) after update, it will be feasible under max=1. The only issue q positive. We can scale to max1; if max=1, score based on S. We just accept if S_new (after scaling) > S. This becomes a constraint feasibility optimization: each update increases total mass under max bound 1. The multiplicative V correction should do that.

Packing algorithm in detail:
```
def central_binom(n):
    q=np.empty(n); q[0]=1
    for k in range(n-1): q[k+1]=q[k]*(2*k+1)/(2*k+2)
```

The KKT correction: We want maximize S subject to C≤1. In barrier method, at optimum for active set w (dual multipliers), ∂L/∂q_i = 1/S - 2 μ ∑ w_k q_{k-i}=0. Thus V_i = 2/S μ ∑ w_k q_{k-i} constant. def packing_search(n, budget_seconds, rng, verbose):
    q = central_binom(n)
    q = np.clip(q,1e-12,None)
    best_q=q.copy(); best_score=score_from_q(q)
    beta=10.0; gamma?; alphas=[0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]
    improve_count=0
    while time < budget:
       C=fft_conv(q,q); M=C.max()
       if M<=0: break
       q = q / np.sqrt(M)
       S=q.sum(); sc=2*n/(S*S)
       if sc<best_score: best_q=q.copy(); best_score=sc; print
       # weights
       # Use active threshold: C >= quantile 99.5? But corner first n=1 (n/(2n-1)≈50%). To include first n, threshold must be near1. Use exp beta(C-1). Since first n C≈1.0. If beta=50, tail weights small.
       diff=C-1.0; diff = np.clip(diff, -1e6, 0)
       w = np.exp(beta*diff); w/=w.sum()  # if underflow all zero due diff maybe? C-1 ≤0; max at 0 ->1.
       # If only one active? okay.
       V=2*cross_hankel(w,q)
       V=np.where(V>1e-300, V, 1e-300)
       base=np.mean(V)
       for alpha in alphas:
          q_new = q * np.exp(alpha*np.log(base/V))
          q_new = np.clip(q_new, 1e-12, None)
          # scale to max1 and check S
          Cnew=fft_conv(q_new,q_new); Mnew=Cnew.max()
          if Mnew>0:
             q_new = q_new / np.sqrt(Mnew)
             Snew=q_new.sum()
          else: continue
          if Snew > S:
             q=q_new; S=Snew; sc=2*n/(S*S); if sc<best: update; break
          else:
             # if no acceptable, after loop beta adjust
       # if no update, change beta / random perturb
       beta = min(200, beta*1.2) if beta<200 else 5
       # occasionally random multiplicative noise
       if rng.random()<0.1:
          q *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.01,n)); rescale
```

Packing search code:
```
def packing_sequence(n, time_budget, rng, verbose_label=''):
    q = central_binom(n)
    best = q.copy()
    best_score = inf
    L = 1 << ((3*n-3).bit_length())
    gamma = 2.0
    alphas_cycle = [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.1]
    ai=0
    while time.monotonic()<end:
       Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
       C=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fq,L, n=2*n-1) # actually irfft returns L, slice
       M=C.max()
       if M<=0: break
       q = q/np.sqrt(M)
       S=q.sum(); sc=2*n/(S*S)
       if sc<best_score: update
       w = C.copy()
       w[w<0]=0
       # emphasize top: w = w**gamma
       # if gamma large, sum underflow? C up to1, so okay.
       w = np.power(w, gamma)
       wsum=w.sum()
       if wsum==0: w[C.argmax()]=1
       else w/=wsum
       W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
       Z=np.fft.irfft(Fq*np.conj(W),L)
       y = np.empty(n)
       idx = precomputed? y[0]=Z[0]; y[1:]=Z[L-n+1:L][::-1]? Need check. idx = (-arange(n))%L. `Z[(-np.arange(n))%L]` creates array; okay.
       V = 2*y.real
       V[V<=0]=1e-300
       base = np.mean(V)
       alpha = alphas_cycle[ai]; ai=(ai+1)%len
       q_new = q * np.exp(alpha*np.log(base/V))
       q_new = np.clip(q_new, 1e-12, None)
       # evaluate and scale
       Fq2=np.fft.rfft(q_new,L)
       Cnew=np.fft.irfft(Fq2*Fq2,L)[:2*n-1]
       Mnew=Cnew.max()
       if Mnew>0:
          scale = 1/np.sqrt(Mnew)
          q_new *= scale
          Snew=q_new.sum()
       else: continue
       if Snew > S or (Snew >= S and rng.random()<0.2):  # accept slight? only if >
           q=q_new
           # do not update best until next score? compute scnew
           sc=2*n/(Snew*Snew)
           if sc<best_score: update
       # If no improve, maybe change gamma or random
       gamma = gamma*1.15 if gamma<50 else 1.0
       # Add tiny noise
       if rng.random() < 0.05:
          q *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.005,n)); q/= sqrt(maxconv?)
    return best/local best normalized p
```

- Random binary sequences with m≈n/2, normalized/scaled to Cmax=1. Use packing update to increase S. However with binary, q_i zero; our multiplicative update uses q_i positive; need add small epsilon to all to allow mass growth. Start q = random 0/1 + eps.
- Use sequences from finite field difference sets: For length n, generate a Paley difference set? But for sums not differences. We can generate random masks and accept only if score <2.
- Use sinusoidal dense p: q_i = 1 + A cos(2π i/(n-1)+φ) normalized, A up to 0.9. p positive. This can create pair sum reduction? Use random parameters and feed to packing.
- Use height_sequence_1 if exists.

The packing update can start from arbitrary q>0 and should increase S toward local optimum. It uses constraints C≤1. Starting dense with Cmax normalized, S might already >√n if score<2; if score=2, S=√n. It will try to increase. If local optimum plateau, random restarts.

Let's detail `improve_q`:
```
def improve_q(q, n, budget, rng, verbose=False):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).copy()
    if q.size != n: truncate/interpolate? We'll generate correct lengths.
    q = np.clip(q, 1e-12, None)
    L = 1 << max(1, (3*n-3).bit_length())
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int); idx[0]=0; idx[1:] = L-1; idx[1:] = L - np.arange(1,n)  # L-1,L-2...
    # Or `idx = (-np.arange(n)) % L`
    best_q = q.copy(); best_score = inf
    gamma = 2.0
    alpha = 0.1
    stagnation = 0
    no_improve_iters = 0
    end = time.monotonic()+budget
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       q = q[nan_to etc]
       Fq = rfft(q,L)
       C = irfft(Fq*Fq,L)[:N]
       M=C.max()
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       # due direct? use if M negative? max positive.
       q = q/np.sqrt(M)
       S=q.sum()
       if S <=0: break
       score = 2*n/(S*S)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_q=q.copy(); no_improve=0; if verbose print
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       # Cycle gamma
       # choose alpha maybe based on no_improve.
       # Weights:
       Cpos=np.maximum(C,0)
       # To avoid normalizing with tiny sum, if gamma too high and only one lag, okay.
       # Use gamma schedule: ensure not all weights equal? gamma values [1,2,5,10,20,40]
       w = Cpos ** gamma
       wsum = w.sum()
       if wsum <=0: w[C.argmax()]=1; wsum=1
       else w/=wsum
       W = rfft(w,L)
       Z = irfft(Fq * np.conjugate(W), L)
       V = 2.0 * Z[idx]
       V = np.where(np.isfinite(V)&(V>0), V, 1e-12)
       base = np.mean(V)
       # alpha candidate maybe adapt
       # Use logarithmic correction with small alpha; if V/base huge, exp could under/over. Alpha max 0.2.
       corr = np.log(base / V)
       corr = np.clip(corr, -5, 5)  # avoid extreme
       q_candidates = []
       for a in (alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*2):
          q_new = q * np.exp(a*corr)
          q_new = np.clip(q_new, 1e-12, None)
          # evaluate quickly
          Fq2 = rfft(q_new,L)
          Cnew = irfft(Fq2*Fq2,L)[:N]
          Mnew=Cnew.max()
          if Mnew>0 and np.isfinite(Mnew):
             q_new_scaled = q_new/np.sqrt(Mnew)
             Snew=q_new_scaled.sum()
             if Snew>S*(1+1e-12):
                 q = q_new_scaled
                 score = 2*n/(Snew*Snew)
                 if score < best_score: update
                 no_improve=0
                 # maybe increase alpha slightly
                 alpha = min(0.4, alpha*1.05)
                 break
          # else try next
       else:
          # no improvement
          gamma = gamma * 1.25
          if gamma > 80: gamma = 1.0
          alpha *= 0.8
          if alpha < 0.01: alpha=0.05
          # stagnation random restart from best
          if no_improve > 200:
             q = best_q * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.02,n))
             q = np.clip(q,1e-12,None)
             no_improve=0
          elif no_improve > 50:
             q = best_q * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.005,n))
             q = np.clip(q,1e-12,None)
    # final rescale best_q to Cmax=1? It already.
    return best_q, best_score
```

```
q = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
q[0]=1.0
for k in range(n-1):
    q[k+1] = q[k] * (2.0*k + 1.0) / (2.0*k + 2.0)
return q
```

Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L)

M=C.max()
S=q.sum()
if S <= 0 or M <=0: return inf
return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```

```
def resample_to_length(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    if x.size == n: return x
    if x.size <2: return np.full(n, x.sum()/max(1,n)?)
    old = np.linspace(0,1,x.size)
    new = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    y = np.interp(new, old, x)
    y = np.clip(y,0,None)
    if y.sum()<=0: y=np.ones(n)
    return y
```

import numpy as np, time, math

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def central_binom(n): ...

def resample_to_length(x,n): ...

def fft_score(q,n,L): ...

def improve_q(q,n,budget,rng,verbose_label=''):
   q = np.asarray(q,float).copy()
   if q.size != n: q=resample_to_length(q,n)
   q=np.clip(q,1e-12,None)
   N=2*n-1
   L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   idx=(-np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64))%L
   end = time.monotonic()+max(0,budget)
   # rescale q to max1
   best_q=None; best_score=np.inf
   gamma=2.0; alpha=0.15; stagnant=0; iter_count=0; last_print=0
   while time.monotonic()<end:
      q=... clip
      Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
      C=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fq,L,N)[:N] # Does irfft accept N? signature irfft(a, n=None, axes...). yes `irfft(..., n=L)` then slice; use n=L to output length L.
      M=float(np.max(C))
      if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0: break
      q=q/np.sqrt(M)
      S=float(np.sum(q))
      if S<=0: break
      score=2.0*n/(S*S)
      if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_q=q.copy(); stagnant=0
      else: stagnant +=1
      if verbose_label and (iter_count % 2000==0): print...
      if time.monotonic() > end: break
      # weights
      Cpos = C.copy(); Cpos[Cpos<0]=0
      # gamma clip
      w = np.power(Cpos, gamma)
      wsum=w.sum()
      if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum<=0:
          w=np.zeros_like(C); int(C.argmax() if C.size else 0); w[argmax]=1; wsum=1
      else: w/=wsum
      W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
      Z=np.fft.irfft(Fq*np.conjugate(W),L)
      V=2.0*Z[idx]
      V=np.where(np.isfinite(V)&(V>0),V,1e-12)
      base=float(np.mean(V))
      if base<=0 or not finite: base=1
      corr=np.log(base/V)
      corr=np.clip(corr,-4,4)
      accepted=False
      for a_mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
          a=alpha*a_mult
          q_new=q*np.exp(a*corr)
          q_new=np.clip(q_new,1e-12,None)
          Fq2=np.fft.rfft(q_new,L)
          C2=np.fft.irfft(Fq2*Fq2,L,N)[:N]
          M2=float(np.max(C2))
          if np.isfinite(M2) and M2>0:
              q_new=q_new/np.sqrt(M2)
              S2=float(np.sum(q_new))
              if S2 > S*(1-1e-7):
                  q=q_new; S=S2
                  score2=2*n/(S*S)
                  if score2 < best_score:
                      best_score=score2; best_q=q.copy(); stagnant=0
                  if S2 > S_old? Need store S_old before loop.
                  alpha=min(0.35,alpha*1.02) if S2>S*(before) else alpha*0.95
                  accepted=True; break
      if not accepted:
          gamma=gamma*1.3
          if gamma>100: gamma=1.0
          alpha=max(0.02, alpha*0.85)
      # stagnation moves
      if stagnant > 80:
          # perturb best
          noise=0.02 if stagnant>200 else 0.005
          q=best_q * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
          q=np.clip(q,1e-12,None)
          # block move sometimes
      elif stagnant > 30:
          # block
          width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//500), max(3,n//20)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
          factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
          qb=q.copy(); qb[start:start+width]*=factor; qb=np.clip(qb,1e-12,None)

      iter_count +=1

   # final scale to Cmax maybe 1? already

Let's write `fft_score` with `n` and `L`:
```
def fft_score(q, n, L):
    q=np.ascontiguousarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    S=float(q.sum())
    if S<=0: return np.inf
    F=np.fft.rfft(q, n=L)
    C=np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=L)[:2*n-1]
    if C.size==0: return np.inf
    M=float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
For q central, F length L. Good.

```

```
def resample_to_length(x,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
   if x.size==n: return x
   if x.size==0: return np.ones(n)
   if x.size==1: return np.full(n,float(x[0]))
   # ensure nonnegative? preserve signs? We clip later.
   old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,x.size)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   y=np.interp(new,old,x)
   return y
```

```python
"""
Algorithm summary:
- Work on nonnegative sequences q of length n, scale-invariantly normalized so
  max(q*q) = 1. For this normalization the evaluator score is 2n / (sum q)^2,
  so improving the sequence is equivalent to maximizing sum(q) subject to
  all convolution coefficients of q*q being at most 1.
- Start from strong seeds:
  * central-binomial sequence q_k = binom(2k,k)/4^k, whose square coefficients
    are flat at 1 for the first n lags;
  * the provided height_sequence_1 (resampled if useful);
  * dense random/binary/sinusoidal/ramp seeds.
- For each length, pre-screen seeds by exact FFT convolution score, then run
  a multiplicative feasibility-improvement loop (a projected natural-gradient
  style method) on the rapidly changing sequence:
      q <- q * exp(alpha * log(mean(V) / V)),  where
      V_i = 2 * sum_j q_j w_{i+j}
  and w are weights emphasizing near-maximum convolution coefficients.
  Each trial is rescaled to max(q*q)=1 and accepted if sum(q) increases,
  i.e. score decreases.  Stagnation triggers gamma changes, smaller/larger
  steps, block scaling, and restarts around the incumbent best.
- Keep the best sequence over several lengths and return it.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _central_binom(n):
    """
    q_k = C(2k,k)/4^k for k=0,...,n-1.
    This is the finite solution whose squared polynomial coefficients are
    exactly 1 for the first n coefficients.
    """
    n = int(n)
    q = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return q
    q[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(n - 1):
        # q_{k+1} / q_k = (2k+1)/(2k+2)
        q[k + 1] = q[k] * float(2 * k + 1) / float(2 * k + 2)
    return q


def _resample_to_length(x, n):
    """Linearly resample 1D array x to length n."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if x.size == n:
        return x
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(x[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(new, old, x)
    return y


def _safe_positive(q, eps=1e-12):
    """Convert q to a finite nonnegative vector with a tiny floor."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, eps, None)
    if q.size == 0 or float(np.sum(q)) <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
    return q


def _fft_conv(q, n, L):
    """Full convolution q*q using FFT length L."""
    F = np.fft.rfft(q, n=L)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F * F, n=L)
    return C[: (2 * n - 1)]


def _fft_score(q, n, L):
    """Evaluator score for q (scale invariant): 2n*max(q*q)/(sum q)^2."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    S = float(np.sum(q))
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    C = _fft_conv(q, n, L)
    M = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def _add_initial(items, q, label):
    """Append an initial candidate with a unique tie-breaker."""
    items.append((len(items), str(label), np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()))


def _build_initial_list(n, rng, hs, global_best):
    """
    Build a diverse candidate list for this length.
    """
    n = int(n)
    items = []

    # Strong analytic seed.
    _add_initial(items, _central_binom(n), "central")

    # Plain uniform seed.
    _add_initial(items, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")

    # Provided construction, if available.
    if hs is not None:
        hs = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if hs.size > 0:
            _add_initial(items, _resample_to_length(hs, n), "provided_hi")

    # Current global best, if already found, resampled.
    if global_best is not None:
        gb = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if gb.size > 0:
            _add_initial(items, _resample_to_length(gb, n), "global_best")

    # Binary half-density random seeds.
    for t in range(5):
        r = rng.random(n)
        b = np.where(r < 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).astype(np.float64)
        b[b == 0.0] = 1e-5
        _add_initial(items, b, f"binary_{t}")

    # Random positive dense seeds.
    for t in range(3):
        r = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
        r = r + 1e-5
        _add_initial(items, r, f"randpos_{t}")

    # Sinusoidal windows.
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    amp_list = (0.50, 0.80, 0.95)
    freq_list = (1, 2, 3, 7)
    s = 0
    for amp in amp_list:
        for freq in freq_list:
            phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
            q = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x / float(n) + phase)
            q = np.clip(q, 1e-6, None)
            _add_initial(items, q, f"sin_{s}")
            s += 1

    # Monotone ramps.
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for lam, tag in ((0.5, "ramp05"), (1.0, "ramp10"), (2.0, "ramp20"), (4.0, "ramp40")):
        q = np.exp(-lam * idx / float(max(1, n)))
        _add_initial(items, q, tag)

    return items


def _screen_initials(items, n, L, limit_seconds):
    """
    Score the initial list by FFT and return sorted list of (score, tie, label, q).
    """
    scored = []
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(limit_seconds))
    for tie, label, q in items:
        q = _safe_positive(q)
        if q.size != n:
            q = _resample_to_length(q, n)
        sc = _fft_score(q, n, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), int(tie), str(label), q))
        if time.monotonic() > end:
            break

    # Tuples are (score, unique tie, label, q); unique tie prevents q comparison.
    scored.sort()
    return scored


def _select_top(scored, max_pick=3):
    """Select up to max_pick sequences, ensuring the central seed is included."""
    if not scored:
        return []

    picks = []
    used_labels = set()

    # Prefer central explicitly.
    for item in scored:
        label = item[2]
        if label == "central" or label.startswith("central"):
            picks.append(item)
            used_labels.add(label)
            break

    for item in scored:
        if len(picks) >= max_pick:
            break
        label = item[2]
        if label not in used_labels:
            picks.append(item)
            used_labels.add(label)

    return picks


def _improve_q(q, n, budget, rng, verbose_label=""):
    """
    Constraint-mass maximization for a fixed length n.
    Returns (best_q, best_score), with best_q scaled so max(best_q*best_q)=1
    (up to numerical error), and best_score = 2n/(sum best_q)^2.
    """
    n = int(n)
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget))
    if budget <= 1e-6 or n <= 0:
        qq = _safe_positive(q)
        L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        return qq, _fft_score(qq, n, L)

    qq = _safe_positive(q)
    if qq.size != n:
        qq = _resample_to_length(qq, n)

    N = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    idx = (-np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)) % L

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + budget

    best_q = None
    best_score = np.inf

    gamma = 2.0
    alpha = 0.15
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        q = _safe_positive(qq)

        Fq = np.fft.rfft(q, n=L)
        C = np.fft.irfft(Fq * Fq, n=L)[:N]
        M = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        q = q / np.sqrt(M)
        S = float(np.sum(q))
        if S <= 0.0:
            break

        score = float(2.0 * n / (S * S))
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_q = q.copy()
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        now = time.monotonic()
        if verbose_label and (now - last_print) > 5.0:
            print(
                f"[{verbose_label}] n={n} score={best_score:.6f} "
                f"S={S:.4f} gamma={gamma:.1f} alpha={alpha:.3f} "
                f"t={now - start:.1f}s",
                flush=True,
            )
            last_print = now

        if time.monotonic() >= end - 0.15:
            break

        # Weights for near-maximum constraints.
        Cpos = C.copy()
        Cpos[Cpos < 0.0] = 0.0
        w = np.power(Cpos, gamma)
        wsum = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(C)
            if C.size:
                w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
                wsum = 1.0
        if wsum > 0.0:
            w = w / wsum

        W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
        Z = np.fft.irfft(Fq * np.conjugate(W), n=L)
        V = 2.0 * Z[idx]
        V = np.where((np.isfinite(V)) & (V > 0.0), V, 1e-12)
        base = float(np.mean(V))
        if not np.isfinite(base) or base <= 0.0:
            base = 1.0

        corr = np.log(base / V)
        corr = np.clip(corr, -4.0, 4.0)

        S_old = S
        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            a = alpha * mult
            q_new = q * np.exp(a * corr)
            q_new = np.clip(q_new, 1e-12, None)

            Fq2 = np.fft.rfft(q_new, n=L)
            C2 = np.fft.irfft(Fq2 * Fq2, n=L)[:N]
            M2 = float(np.max(C2)) if C2.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(M2) or M2 <= 0.0:
                continue

            q_new = q_new / np.sqrt(M2)
            S2 = float(np.sum(q_new))
            if not np.isfinite(S2) or S2 <= 0.0:
                continue

            # Accept tiny regressions occasionally to escape numerical plateaus.
            if S2 > S_old * (1.0 - 1e-8):
                q = q_new
                S = S2
                sc2 = float(2.0 * n / (S * S))
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_q = q.copy()
                    stagnant = 0
                alpha = min(0.32, alpha * (1.05 if S2 > S_old else 0.96))
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            gamma *= 1.25
            if gamma > 25.0:
                gamma = 1.5
            alpha = max(0.02, alpha * 0.88)

        # Stagnation handles.
        if best_q is not None and stagnant > 120:
            noise = 0.02 if stagnant > 400 else 0.006
            q = best_q * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            q = _safe_positive(q)
            stagnant = 0

        elif stagnant > 45:
            # Try a block scaling move.
            wmin = max(2, n // 500)
            wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 20)
            width = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax))
            width = max(2, min(width, n - 2))
            start_i = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))

            qb = q.copy()
            qb[start_i:start_i + width] *= factor
            qb = _safe_positive(qb)

            Fb = np.fft.rfft(qb, n=L)
            B = np.fft.irfft(Fb * Fb, n=L)[:N]
            Mb = float(np.max(B)) if B.size else 0.0
            if np.isfinite(Mb) and Mb > 0.0:
                qb = qb / np.sqrt(Mb)
                Sb = float(np.sum(qb))
                if np.isfinite(Sb) and Sb > S:
                    q = qb
                    S = Sb
                    scb = float(2.0 * n / (S * S))
                    if scb < best_score:
                        best_score = scb
                        best_q = q.copy()
                        stagnant = 0

        it += 1

    if best_q is None:
        qq = _safe_positive(q)
        L2 = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        best_q = qq
        best_score = _fft_score(qq, n, L2)

    return best_q, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, seed=42, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for best sequence within the offered time budget.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    total_budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, total_budget - 8.0)

    global_best = None
    global_score = np.inf

    # Access provided construction if present.
    hs = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if hs.size > 0 and np.isfinite(hs).any():
                hs = _safe_positive(hs)
            else:
                hs = None
    except Exception:
        hs = None

    # Configure length/run fractions.
    configs = []
    if hs is not None and 1200 <= hs.size <= 12000:
        configs.append((int(hs.size), 0.28, "provided_length"))
    configs.append((2048, 0.20, "n2048"))
    configs.append((4096, 0.26, "n4096"))
    configs.append((8192, 0.26, "n8192"))

    # Normalize fractions.
    frac_sum = 0.0
    adjusted = []
    for n, f, name in configs:
        if f <= 0:
            continue
        adjusted.append((int(n), float(f), name))
        frac_sum += float(f)

    if frac_sum <= 0.0:
        adjusted = [(4096, 1.0, "n4096")]
        frac_sum = 1.0

    # Deduplicate lengths while keeping fractions.
    seen_lens = set()
    unique_configs = []
    for n, f, name in adjusted:
        if n in seen_lens:
            unique_configs[-1] = (n, unique_configs[-1][1] + f, name)
        else:
            unique_configs.append((n, f, name))
            seen_lens.add(n)

    for n, fr, name in unique_configs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break

        cfg_budget = (total_budget - 8.0) * (fr / frac_sum)
        if cfg_budget < 25.0:
            continue

        n = int(n)
        L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

        # Pre-screen quickly.
        screen_limit = min(12.0, max(2.0, cfg_budget / 18.0))
        items = _build_initial_list(n, rng, hs, global_best)
        scored = _screen_initials(items, n, L, screen_limit)
        picks = _select_top(scored, max_pick=3)

        if not picks:
            # Fallback: central seed.
            picks = [(np.inf, 0, "central", _central_binom(n))]

        used = 0.0
        for sc0, tie, label, q0 in picks:
            remaining = cfg_budget - used
            if remaining < 5.0 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                break
            use = min(remaining, max(5.0, cfg_budget / max(1, len(picks))))
            full_label = f"{name}/{label}"

            q0 = _safe_positive(q0)
            if q0.size != n:
                q0 = _resample_to_length(q0, n)

            q_best, sc_best = _improve_q(q0, n, use, rng, verbose_label=full_label)
            used += time.monotonic() - (time.monotonic() - use)  # placeholder; actual below

            # Compute actual time spent more directly by comparing times.

            if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < global_score:
                global_score = float(sc_best)
                global_best = q_best.copy()

        # If one pick was selected but multiple available, still ensure time
        # accounting did not break. Continue to next length.

    # Fallback exact small construction: p = [8/15, 4/15, 3/15] has score 384/225 ~ 1.7067.
    if global_best is None:
        global_best = np.array([8.0, 4.0, 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
        global_score = 384.0 / 225.0
    else:
        global_best = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if global_best.size == 0:
            global_best = np.array([8.0, 4.0, 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
            global_score = 384.0 / 225.0

    # Final sanitization for evaluator.
    global_best = _safe_positive(global_best, eps=1e-12)
    global_best = np.clip(global_best, 1e-12, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(global_best)) < 0.01:
        global_best = global_best / max(1e-300, float(np.sum(global_best)))
        global_best = np.clip(global_best, 1e-12, 1000.0)

    # Optional authority check.
    final_list = [float(x) for x in global_best]
    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            global_score = ev
        else:
            # If evaluator sees something invalid, fall back to a safe tiny sequence.
            final_list = [8.0, 4.0, 3.0]
            global_score = 384.0 / 225.0
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final_list
```
